def apply_flip(p, oldbit, q10, q01):
    newbit = 1 - oldbit
    if p+1 < n:
       nb = h? But h not passed? Need neighbor values. For update q, we only need old bit and neighbor bits. If h array global or passed. Since we haven't flipped h, neighbor values are current. For p, forward neighbors h[p+1:], backward h[:p].
       f = q10[p+1:]? distance d = q-p corresponds slice index d-1 = (q-p)-1 = p... Actually q10 index distance d-1. For forward pair (p, p+t), d=t, index t-1 ranges p..n-p-2. q10[p : n-p-1] corresponds neighbor q=p+1..n-1. q10[p:n-p-1] length n-p-1. Good.
       For update when bit flips old->new:
       if old==0, new=1:
          forward: neighbor c=0 => pair old (0,0) no; new (1,0) q10 +1. c=1 => old (0,1) q01 +1; new (1,1) none -> q01 -1. So q10[...,c==0]+=1, q01[...,c==1]-=1.
      if old==1,new=0:
          q10 forward c==0 -1; q01 forward c==1 +1.
     For backward pairs (q,p) with q=p-t, i=q, j=p. old h_p=old, neighbor c.
       old==0,new=1:
          c=1: old (1,0) q10=1; new (1,1)0 => q10 -1.
          c=0: old(0,0)0; new(0,1) q01=1 => q01 +1.
       old==1,new=0:
          c=1: old(1,1)0; new(1,0) q10=1 => q10 +1.
          c=0: old(0,1) q01=1; new(0,0)0 => q01 -1.
  Can vectorize with masks. Instead of branching on old, compute formulas.
  For forward with neighbor nb (0/1): old=bit, new=1-bit.
  q10_delta = new*(1-nb) - old*(1-nb) = (new-old)*(1-nb) = (1-2*old)*(1-nb) ? If old=0 => +1 where nb=0; old=1 => -1 where nb=0. yes delta10_f = (1 - 2*old) * (1 - nb).
  q01_delta = (new==0? no q01=old? formula q01=(1-old)*nb? Wait 0 at i,1 at j: indicator = (1-h_i)h_j. For forward i=p. q01 = (1-old)*nb old. New = (1-new)*nb = old*nb. Delta = old*nb - (1-old)*nb = (2*old-1)*nb = -(1-2old)*nb. So delta10_f = s*(1-nb), delta01_f = -s*nb, where s=1-2old (+1 for 0->1, -1 for 1->0).
  For backward i=neighbor c, j=p. q10 = c*(1-old), new c*(1-new)=c*old. Delta = c*(old - (1-old)) = (2old-1)c = -s c. q01 = (1-c)*old, new=(1-c)*new=(1-c)*(1-old). Delta = (1-old)*(1-c) - old*(1-c) = (1-2old)*(1-c)=s*(1-c).
  So apply:
    s = 1 - 2*old
    if p+1<n:
       nb = h[p+1:n]
       q10[p:n-p-1] += s*(1-nb)
       q01[p:n-p-1] += -s*nb
    if p>0:
       nb = h[:p]
       q10[:p] += -s*nb
       q01[:p] += s*(1-nb)
  Need q slices correspond exactly indices 0..p-1 for backward. Good.

Projection: clip [0,1], then adjust sum. Efficient:
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2
    diff=target-h.sum()
    h += diff/n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # if sum off due clipping, iterate water-filling
    while? We can implement water filling exactly:
Need project x onto box and hyperplane sum=t. Algorithm: Start x=clip(target? Actually projection of y onto {0≤h≤1,sum=t}. We can find λ such that sum clip(y-λ,0,1)=t. Binary search λ. But projected gradient step might push many out; iterative 50 okay. Could implement exact via sorting: Sort y desc; find threshold. But n 512, simple binary search better.
Projection(y,t):
  lo=-1e12, hi=1e12? We need sum clip(y-λ,0,1)=t. f(λ)=sum(clip(y-λ,0,1)) decreasing. λ range [min(y)-1, max(y)+1]. Binary 40 iters O(n*40) per step; if 1000 steps*512*40=20M, okay. We can do 30.
  lower = max? Use bisection:
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0  (if λ low, sum near n)
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(40): mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum(); if s>t: lo=mid else hi=mid.
  h=clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1); adjust small.

Let's design continuous projected gradient (PG) algorithm:
- h feasible.
- Compute objective raw C of h; evaluate.
- Smooth objective gβ with weight β (on q, not C). We want descent of g but also raw C. We'll do line search on raw C(g? maybe use raw C for acceptance). Since g approximates raw, descent in g often improves raw.
- Use nonmonotone: keep history g values, accept if f_raw < best local? We'll track best raw.
Pseudo:
def pg_optimize(h, n, time_limit, beta list):
  h=project_feasible(h)
  best=compute_c5(h)
  g_prev=smooth_max(h,n,beta)*scale
  step=0.5
  for warm...:
    f, m, weights = eval_smooth(h,n,beta)
    grad = grad_smooth(h, weights,n)
    # scale grad
    # try lr from step down/up
    accepted=False
    for lr in [step, 0.5*step, 2*step, 4*step]:
       h_new=project(h - lr*grad, n)
       f_new=compute_c5(h_new)
       if f_new < best - 1e-12 or (f_new < f + 1e-12): # for smooth?
          h=h_new; desired step *= maybe; accepted; break
    if not accepted: step*=0.5
    if best raw improves update.
  return h

def grad_sm(x): q; m; s; w=exp(s)/sum; grad raw (for q) scaled by (2/n) with sum w? For smooth max, gradient = ∑ w ∇q /? Since g = m + (1/β)logsumexp(β(q-m)). Derivative: (1/β)*(β∑ w ∇q - β∑ w *? derivative of (q_l -m) includes ∇q_l - ∑ p ∇q_m? Need derive. If m = max q, not differentiable; but logsumexp with p, define g = (1/β) logsumexp(β q_i). def _smooth_obj_values(h, beta): q = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full'); maxq = q.max(); s = beta*(q-maxq); smax = maxq + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s))) / beta; return smax, q? For objective return smax*2/n.
def _smooth_grad(h, n, beta): compute q, maxq, s, w=exp(s)/sum; then grad = zero; loop active weights > threshold. For each m, d=m-(n-1); if d>=0: grad[:n-d] += w*(1-h[d:]); grad[d:] += -w*h[:n-d]; else o=-d: grad[:n-o] += -w*h[o:]; grad[o:] += w*(1-h[:n-o]). Return (2/n)*grad.

   def apply_flip(h, p, q10, q01):
      old = int(h[p])
      # update q using neighbors based on current h (before flip)
      s = 1 - 2*old
      if p+1<n:
         nb = h[p+1:n]
         if s>0: # old 0->1
            q10[p:n-p-1] += 1 - nb
            q01[p:n-p-1] -= nb
         else:
            q10[p:n-p-1] -= 1 - nb
            q01[p:n-p-1] += nb
      if p>0:
         nb=h[:p]
         if s>0:
            q10[:p] -= nb
            q01[:p] += 1 - nb
         else:
            q10[:p] += nb
            q01[:p] -= 1 - nb
      newbit = 1-old
      h[p] = newbit
      return newbit
This updates h inside. For revert, call apply_flip(h,p) again? That flips back but we need ensure q delta correct: it uses current h neighbors (which after full candidate may include other flips). Simulated annealing loop:
def binary_sa_optimize(h0, n, initial_t, time_limit, best_params):
   h=h0.copy(); q10,q01=compute_q(h); K=...
   best_h=h.copy(); best_K=K; best_C=2K/n
   t=initial_t
   no_imp=0; iterations=0
   while time-left>... and ...:
      # propose swap
      p=randint(n); q=randint(n)
      if h[p]==h[q]: continue (maybe resample few)
      oldK=K
      # apply p, q
      apply_flip(h,p,q10,q01); apply_flip(h,q,q10,q01)
      newK = int(max(q10.max(), q01.max()))
      if newK < oldK:
         accept_count +=1; K=newK; no_imp=0; if K<best_K update best
      elif newK == oldK:
         if random < 0.3: K=newK
         else: revert; K=oldK
      else:
         # accept with prob exp(-(newK-oldK)/t) (t count scale)
         if random < exp(-(newK-oldK)/t): accept; K=newK; no_imp? maybe increase
         else revert.
      # cool t *= 0.999?
      if no_imp > threshold: t = max(0.1, t*1.5?) kick.
   return best_h,best_C

Implement smooth objective and jac:
```
_S_N = 0
_S_BETA = 1.0

def _smooth_obj_jac(x, beta, return_values=True):
    n=_S_N
    q = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full').astype(float)
    maxq = q.max()
    s = beta*(q-maxq)
    # under/overflow clip
    s = np.clip(s, -700, 700) # for high beta, huge; if maxq large? okay
    exps = np.exp(s)
    z = exps.sum()
    w = exps/z
    sm = maxq + np.log(z)/beta # since shift by max, logsumexp(beta(q-max))/beta + max
    val = sm * (2.0/n)
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    # maybe active = w > 1e-12
    # To speed, loop all m, if w small skip
    for m, wi in enumerate(w):
       if wi <= 1e-12: continue
       d = m - (n-1)
       if d >= 0:
          grad[:n-d] += wi * (1.0 - x[d:])
          grad[d:]  += -wi * x[:n-d]
       else:
          o = -d
          grad[:n-o] += -wi * x[o:]
          grad[o:]  += wi * (1.0 - x[:n-o])
    return val, grad*(2.0/n)
```
Wait scaling: If val = sm*(2/n), gradient of SCALE * logsumexp/β is SCALE * w·∇q. sm = logsumexp/β (with shift? actually sm=logsumexp(beta q)/beta). Derivative = w·∇q. So return val, (2/n)*grad. We don't need max shift in val? We computed sm=maxq + log(z)/beta, which equals logsumexp(q shifted)/beta (since shift zero). Good.
But if beta high, s for non-max large negative; w only active. Good.
Potential underflow: if s=-1000, exp 0, okay; clip at -745 maybe. Use -700.
Constraint: `_eq_con(x)` sum - n/2; jac ones.

But SLSQP calls objective multiple times; we need separate function for objective only? It expects jac returns (f,g). Good.

Let's implement:
```
def binary_loads(h):
   n=len(h); q10=np.zeros(n-1,np.int16); q01=...
   for d in range(1,n):
      left = h[:n-d]
      right = h[d:]
      q10[d-1] = np.count_nonzero((left == 1) & (right == 0))
      q01[d-1] = np.count_nonzero((left == 0) & (right == 1))
   return q10,q01
```
This uses h int8, okay. For n=2048, 2M C, fine.

But note if h is int8 shape, `left==1` bool. Good.

Thus compute_c5:
```
def compute_c5(h):
   n=len(h)
   if n<=1: return 0.0
   hf = h if float? h float. b=1-h
   c10 = np.convolve(hf, b[::-1])
   # max over d=0..n-1 at c10[0:n]
   m1 = np.max(c10[:n]) if n>0 else 0
   c01 = np.convolve(b, hf[::-1])
   m2 = np.max(c01[:n])
   return float(max(m1,m2) * (2.0/n))
```
But note c10 length 2n-1; first n values index 0..n-1 correspond d=n-1..0. max over first n includes all nonnegative lags and 0. good.
Check for h constant 0.5 length4: b=0.5. c10 = convolution of 0.5s: [0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,0.75,0.5,0.25]; first4 max1.0, d0=1? For n=4, h(1-h) sum=4*0.25=1, C=2/4*1=0.5. C_result=1*0.5=0.5. Good.

Let's design SLSQP optimization:
```
def slsqp_polish(h0, n, time_limit, betas, maxiters):
    global _S_N, _S_BETA
    _S_N = n
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best = compute_c5(h)
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_con,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    for beta in betas:
       if time expired: break
       _S_BETA = float(beta)
       maxiter = ... choose based on remaining and n
       try:
          res = minimize(_obj_jac, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                         options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-14, 'iprint':0})
          h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
       except: pass
       c=compute_c5(h); if c<best: best=c
    return h,best
```

Parent interpolation function:
```
def interpolate_to(h, p_n, n):
   if p_n == n: return h.copy()
   xp = (np.arange(p_n)+0.5)*2.0/p_n
   xn = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
   return np.interp(xn, xp, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
```

```
def binary_sa_optimize(h0, n, time_limit, target_K=None, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = h0.copy().astype(np.int8)
    # ensure sum M
    M = n//2
    s = h.sum()
    if s != M: adjust: if s>M set s-M ones to0 with smallest? For binary init exact but do.
    q10,q01 = compute_binary_loads(h)
    K = int(max(q10.max(), q01.max()))
    best_h = h.copy(); best_K=K; best_C = 2*best_K/n
    t = 1.0
    it=0; last_imp_it=0; active=None; active_timer=0
    while time.time()... > start + time_limit:
       # if active_timer > 2000 or active is None: rebuild
       if active is None or active_timer > 2000:
          active = build_active_positions(h,q10,q01,K)
          active_timer=0
       active_timer +=1
       it+=1
       # propose p
       if use_target and active is not None and len(active)>0 and rng.rand()<0.7:
          p = int(active[rng.randint(len(active))])
       else:
          p = rng.randint(n)
       bitp = h[p]
       # find q opposite
       q = -1
       for _ in range(20):
           q = rng.randint(n)
           if q != p and h[q] != bitp: break
       else:
           # fallback find
           # build list? rare
           q = rng.randint(n)
           if h[q]==bitp or q==p: continue
       oldK=K
       apply_flip(h,p,q10,q01)
       apply_flip(h,q,q10,q01)
       newK = int(max(q10.max(), q01.max()))
       accept = False
       if newK < oldK:
          accept = True
       elif newK == oldK:
          accept = rng.rand() < 0.25
       else:
          # SA acceptance; avoid large jumps
          diff = newK - oldK
          if diff <= 4:
             accept = rng.rand() < math.exp(-float(diff)/t)
          else:
             accept = False
       if accept:
          K = newK
          if K < best_K:
             best_K=K; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=2*K/n; last_imp_it=it
          # if improved, maybe rebuild active immediately
          active_timer=2000
       else:
          apply_flip(h,q,q10,q01)
          apply_flip(h,p,q10,q01)
          K=oldK
       # cooling
       if it - last_imp_it > 3000:
          t = min(5.0, t*1.5)
          # random kick: accept equal more? reset active
          active=None; active_timer=0
       else:
          t = max(0.1, t*0.998)
       if best_C <= (2*target_K/n?) maybe break? If target achieved? We can stop n search.
    return best_h,best_C
```

def build_active_positions(h,q10,q01,K):
    n=len(h)
    parts=[]
    if K<=0: return np.empty(0,int)
    # top indices where q10 >= K-1 maybe plus if all lower
    topk = min(20, q10.size)
    # use argpartition for indices with high values
    # Need include positions only if load close to max
    idx10 = np.argpartition(q10, -topk)[-topk:] maybe if topk < size.
    for idx in idx10:
       val=q10[idx]; if val < max(1, K-2): continue
       d=idx+1; L=n-d
       cand = np.nonzero((h[:L]==1)&(h[d:]==0))[0]
       parts.append(cand)
    # q01: zeros with right one
    idx01=...
       cand = np.nonzero((h[:L]==0)&(h[d:]==1))[0]
    if parts: return np.concatenate(parts)
    # if none, return nonzero h? maybe random
    return np.flatnonzero(h)  # maybe all ones? We can return all positions to choose random.
```

def slsqp_polish(h0, n, time_limit):
   start=time.time(); rem = time_limit
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   best=compute_c5(h)
   betas = [5.0, 50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 20000.0]
   maxit_base = 60 if n>350 else 100 if n>220 else 150
   for beta in betas:
      remaining = time_limit-(time.time()-start)
      if remaining < 15: break
      maxit = maxit_base
      # If remaining large and beta high, maybe more:
      if beta>=5000 and remaining>60: maxit=2*maxit_base
      _S_N=n; _S_BETA=beta
      res=minimize(... options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-12})
      ... update best
   return h,best
```

Now exact sum fix:
Project water filling:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    t = 0.5*n
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    # quick clip
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = y.sum()
    if abs(s-t) < 1e-12:
       return y
    # binary search lambda
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    # f(lambda)=sum(clip(y-lambda,0,1)); f(lo) ~ n, f(hi)~0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s = np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > t:
          lo = mid
       else:
          hi = mid
    h = np.clip(y-(lo+hi),0,1)
    # fix tiny sum error
    diff = t - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
       idx = np.argmax(1.0 - h)
       add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
       h[idx] += add
       diff -= add
       if diff>0:
          # distribute to several
          order=np.argsort(1.0-h)
          for i in order:
             add = min(diff,1.0-h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add; if diff<=0: break
    elif diff <0:
       idx=np.argmax(h); sub=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=sub; ...
    # final adjust maybe
    diff = t - h.sum()
    if diff != 0:
       if diff>0: order sorted spec distances; else
       for i in order: ...
    return h
```
Could be heavy but only per optimization stage. 50* n ops. For n=512, 25k, fine.
Need if y already infeasible due to all out? ok.

Maybe the waterfill projection requires more iterations? 50 enough for double.

Now function `round_to_binary_top(h,n)`:
```
def round_to_binary_top(h,n):
   M=n//2
   if M<=0: return zeros
   # To avoid all ties, if n large use argpartition
   part = np.argpartition(h, n-M)  # n-M=M since half? if n even.
   top = part[M:]
   # Ensure exactly M? If ties, maybe top size M.
   b=np.zeros(n,np.int8); b[top]=1
   s=b.sum()
   if s>M:
      # turn off smallest h among ones
      order=np.argsort(h[b==1])? can't index bool for argsort easily
      idx=np.where(b==1)[0]; order=idx[np.argsort(h[idx])][:s-M]; b[order]=0
   elif s<M:
      idx=np.where(b==0)[0]; order=idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])][:M-s]; b[order]=1
   return b
```
If h has NaN? no.

`binary_loads_int` via conv:
```
def binary_loads_int(h):
   n=len(h); hf=h.astype(float)
   b=1.0-hf
   c10=np.convolve(hf, b[::-1])
   c01=np.convolve(b, hf[::-1])
   q10=np.zeros(max(n-1,0), dtype=np.int16)
   q01=np.zeros(max(n-1,0), dtype=np.int16)
   if n>1:
      q10[:] = np.rint(c10[:n-1][::-1])
      q01[:] = np.rint(c01[:n-1][::-1])
   q10=np.clip(q10,0,n).astype(np.int16)
   q01=np.clip(q01,0,n).astype(np.int16)
   return q10,q01
```
Convolution with float may introduce -1e-15; clip.

Now `apply_flip` must know n from len h. It modifies q10/q01. But if h not int8? We'll ensure int8.
```
def apply_flip(h, p, q10, q01):
    n = len(h)
    old = int(h[p])
    if old == 0:
       L = n - p - 1
       if L > 0:
          nb = h[p+1:]
          q10[:L] += 1 - nb
          q01[:L] -= nb
       P = p
       if P > 0:
          nb = h[:P][::-1]
          q10[:P] -= nb
          q01[:P] += 1 - nb
       h[p] = 1
    else:
       L = n - p - 1
       if L > 0:
          nb = h[p+1:]
          q10[:L] -= 1 - nb
          q01[:L] += nb
       P = p
       if P > 0:
          nb = h[:P][::-1]
          q10[:P] += nb
          q01[:P] -= 1 - nb
       h[p] = 0
```
Since q10 slice length L/P <= n-1. For p=0, P=0 skip; L=n-1 q10[:n-1] length ok. For p=n-1, L=0; P=n-1 q10[:n-1].

Potential issue: In-place addition on overlapping slices when backward and forward update same q arrays; we do them sequentially; correct. For old=0, forward q10[:L]+=, backward q10[:P]-=; if L and P overlap, both applied. Fine.

But note `1 - nb` when nb is a view of h int8 returns a new int8 array? It should. For speed, can avoid by temp but okay.

```
def make_binary_starts(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng, max_starts):
    starts=[]
    M=n//2
    if parent_h is not None:
       if parent_n == n: hcont=parent_h.astype(float)
       else: hcont=interpolate_to(parent_h,parent_n,n)
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(hcont,n))
    # random
    perm=rng.permutation(n); rb=np.zeros(n,np.int8); rb[perm[:M]]=1; starts.append(rb)
    if max_starts>=3:
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
       hsin=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*2.0*x + rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(hsin,n))
    if max_starts>=4:
       # triangular
       ht=0.5 + 0.35*np.cos(np.pi*x) #? project top
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(ht,n))
    return starts[:max_starts]
```

Now think about continuous objective gradient with convolve. Let's code `_smooth_obj_jac` carefully.
```
def _smooth_obj_jac(x, n, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - x
    c10 = np.convolve(x, b[::-1])
    c01 = np.convolve(b, x[::-1])
    # P[d] = c10[n-1-d], d=0..n-1
    # vals length 2n-1: vals[0]=P0, vals[1+n? let's create]
    vals = np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    # P[0:n] reverse c10[:n]
    P = c10[:n][::-1]
    N = c01[:n][::-1]
    vals[0] = P[0]
    vals[1:n+1] = P[1:] # length n-1
    vals[n+1:] = N[1:] # length n-1
```
Length: 1 + (n-1) + (n-1) =2n-1. `vals[n+1:]` index from n+1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n-1. For n=2: vals len3; vals[0]=P0, vals[1:3]=P1, vals[3:] empty; okay.
But for n=1, len1; handle? n maybe >1. For n=1, no correlation? C=0 maybe. But problem n? we can ensure n>1.
Max `vmax = vals.max()`; `s = beta*(vals-vmax)`; clip; e; w=e/expsum; smooth = vmax + log(expsum)/beta; val=smooth*(2/n).
Gradient:
```
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    w0 = w[0]
    if w0>1e-14: grad += w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    # positive d=1..n-1 weights w[1:d?] d index in vals = d
    for d in range(1,n):
       wi = w[d]
       if wi <= 1e-12: continue
       L = n-d
       grad[:L] += wi*(1.0 - x[d:])
       grad[d:] += -wi*x[:L]
       wj = w[n+d]  # negative d index? vals index for N[d] is n+d? For d=1, index n+1? Wait vals layout: vals[1:n+1]=P; P[d] at index d for d=1..n-1. vals[n+1:]=N[1:]; N[1] at index n+1. So N[d] at index n+d? For d=1 -> n+1 yes. For d=n-1 -> 2n-2 yes. Good.
       if wj > 1e-12:
          grad[:L] += -wj*x[d:]
          grad[d:] += wj*(1.0 - x[:L])
    return val, grad*(2.0/n)
```
Could combine positive/negative in one loop over d.

However, loop `for d in range(1,n)` with vector slices for every d even if weights small. For high beta, active few; but still loop n. For n=512 and SLSQP 300 =>153k loops, okay. If weights all small threshold skip? If beta low, many weights >1e-12; loop full. Okay.

Potential issue: For low beta, `w[d]` all ~1/(2n) ~0.002, loop 512*512 operations =262k per gradient. Good.

Can we compute gradient faster using convolution of weights? The weighted sum of shifted (1-x) can be computed via convolve with weights? Maybe but loop fine.

SLSQP objective value `val` smooth max of q's, not exactly raw; for low beta near average. Stages.

   nconv=2*n; f1=fft(x,nconv); f2=fft(b[::-1],nconv); c10= ifft(f1*f2).real[:2n-1]. This is O(n log n). For n=512 direct okay, but FFT may be similar. Need ensure precision. Could use for n>256. However, `np.fft` complex overhead maybe. Simpler keep direct; n limited.

Let's implement `project_feasible` efficiently:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5*n
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n == 0: return y
    s = y.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-12:
       # fix tiny? maybe
       y = y.copy()
    else:
       lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
       hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
       for _ in range(60):
          mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
          s = np.clip(y-mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
          if s > target:
             lo = mid
          else:
             hi = mid
       y = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if diff > 0:
       # add to elements with room; if small, first argmax
       if diff < 1e-12:
          pass
       else:
          idx = np.argmax(1.0-y)
          add = min(diff, max(1.0-y[idx],0.0))
          y[idx] += add
          diff -= add
          if diff > 0:
             order = np.argsort(1.0-y)
             for i in order:
                if diff <= 0: break
                add = min(diff, max(1.0-y[i],0.0))
                y[i] += add
                diff -= add
    elif diff < 0:
       neg = -diff
       idx = np.argmax(y)
       sub = min(neg, max(y[idx],0.0))
       y[idx] -= sub
       neg -= sub
       if neg > 0:
          order=np.argsort(y)
          for i in order:
             if neg<=0: break
             sub=min(neg, max(y[i],0.0)); y[i]-=sub; neg-=sub
    return y
```
If adding to fill and diff residual > max room? target between0,n, should fill. For n integer target maybe .5 if odd, okay.

`round_to_binary_top` for odd n? We only call for even. If n odd, M=n//2, but sum target n/2 not integer; binary can't. We'll call only if n%2==0. For odd continuous, no round.

`make_binary_starts` should handle n even.
```
def make_binary_starts(parent_h,parent_n,n,rng,max_starts):
    M=n//2
    starts=[]
    if parent_h is not None:
       if parent_n == n:
          pc=parent_h.astype(float).copy()
       else:
          pc=interpolate_to(parent_h.astype(float), parent_n, n)
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(pc,n))
    starts.append(random_binary(n,rng))
    if max_starts >=3:
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
       phase = (rng.rand()*2.0*np.pi)
       hv=0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*2.0*x + phase)
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(hv,n))
    if max_starts >=4:
       hv=0.5 + 0.30*np.cos(np.pi*x) # maybe endpoint? Use sin(π x) zero ends?
       starts.append(round_to_binary_top(hv,n))
    return starts[:max_starts]
```
`x` defined only if max>=3; ensure.

`random_binary`:
```
def random_binary(n,rng):
   b=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8)
   idx=rng.choice(n, size=n//2, replace=False)
   b[idx]=1
   return b
```
`rng.choice` with replace=False for n up to512 fine. Or permutation for speed.

   return project_feasible(0.5 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + phase), n)

def ensure_binary_sum(h,n,rng):

   if s>M:
      idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
      if len(idx)==0: return h
      order=idx[np.argsort(rng.rand(len(idx)))]? To choose arbitrary. Use order=idx[rng.permutation(len(idx))][:s-M]; h[order]=0
   elif s<M: ...

In binary_sa:
```
def binary_sa_optimize(h0,n,time_limit,seed=1,active_period=1000):
   if n<4 or n%2!=0: return None? But call even.
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed*1000003+n)
   h=np.asarray(h0,dtype=np.int8).copy()
   h=ensure_binary_sum(h,n,rng)
   q10,q01=binary_loads_int(h)
   if q10.size==0: K=0
   else: K=int(max(q10.max(), q01.max()))
   best_h=h.copy(); best_K=K; best_C=2.0*K/n
   end=time.time()+time_limit
   t=2.0
   last_imp=0; it=0; active=None; active_timer=0
   active_period=800
   while time.time() < end:
      it+=1
      if active is None or active_timer>=active_period:
         active=build_active_positions(h,q10,q01,K)
         active_timer=0
      else: active_timer+=1
      if active is not None and len(active)>0 and (it % 10 < 7):
         p=int(active[rng.randint(len(active))])
      else:
         p=rng.randint(n)
      bitp=int(h[p])
      q=-1
      for _ in range(30):
         q=int(rng.randint(n))
         if q != p and int(h[q]) != bitp:
            break
      else:
         # fallback: find any opposite from array
         if bitp==0:
            zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0); ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1)
            if len(ones)==0: break
            q=int(ones[0]); if q==p: continue
         else:
            zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
            if len(zeros)==0: break
            q=int(zeros[0]); if q==p: continue
      oldK=K
      apply_flip(h,p,q10,q01)
      apply_flip(h,q,q10,q01)
      m1=int(q10.max()) if q10.size else 0
      m2=int(q01.max()) if q01.size else 0
      newK = m1 if m1>=m2 else m2
      accept=False
      if newK < oldK:
         accept=True
      elif newK == oldK:
         # accept some equal; if we have been doing many equal, maybe reduce
         accept = rng.rand() < 0.2
      else:
         diff = newK-oldK
         if diff <= 6:
            accept = rng.rand() < math.exp(-float(diff)/t)
      if accept:
         K = newK
         if K < best_K:
            best_K=K; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=2.0*K/n; last_imp=it; active_timer=active_period
      else:
         apply_flip(h,q,q10,q01)
         apply_flip(h,p,q10,q01)
         K=oldK
      # cooling
      if it - last_imp > 2000:
         t = min(6.0, t*1.2)
         active=None; active_timer=0
      elif K > best_K:
         t = max(0.5, t) # maybe high
      else:
         t = max(0.1, t*0.995)
   return best_h,best_C
```
Potential bug: If we accept equal move, K same but best maybe; last_imp not updated. If many equal, triggers kick. Good.
If q not found, `break` while? Need `continue` to next iteration? If no opposite impossible because half ones; but if h sum not fixed maybe; after ensure sum M, there is. In fallback, if break from for-else, need `continue` not break outer. We'll structure:
```

```
def build_active_positions(h,q10,q01,K):
   n=len(h)
   if K<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
   parts=[]
   top = min(8, q10.size)
   if q10.size:
      if top == q10.size:
         idx10=np.argsort(q10)[::-1]
      else:
         idx10=np.argpartition(q10, -top)[-top:]
      for idx in idx10:
         val=int(q10[idx])
         if val < max(1, K-2): continue
         d=int(idx)+1; L=n-d
         cand=np.nonzero((h[:L]==1) & (h[d:]==0))[0]
         if cand.size: parts.append(cand)
   if q01.size:
      top=min(8,q01.size)
      idx01=...
      for idx in idx01:
         val=int(q01[idx]); if val < max(1,K-2): continue
         d=idx+1; L=n-d
         cand=np.nonzero((h[:L]==0) & (h[d:]==1))[0]
         if cand.size: parts.append(cand)
   if parts:
      arr=np.concatenate(parts)
      if arr.size>0:
         # unique? not necessary but to avoid huge duplicates perhaps
         return arr
   return np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64)
```
`np.argpartition(q10, -top)` if top=size? We handle. q10 int16.

Potential performance: `np.nonzero` with boolean arrays each build for top lags. Good.

Now `slsqp_polish` code with bounds. Need maybe convert h to float and project.
```
def slsqp_polish(h0,n,time_limit):
    global _S_N,_S_BETA
    start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best=compute_c5(h)
    if n<2: return h,best
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_con,'jac':_eq_jac}]
    betas=[5.0, 50.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0]
    if n>380:
       maxits=[40,60,80,100,120]
    elif n>240:
       maxits=[60,80,100,120,140]
    else:
       maxits=[80,100,120,150,180]
    for beta, mi in zip(betas,maxits):
       rem = time_limit - (time.time()-start)
       if rem < 12: break
       _S_N=n; _S_BETA=float(beta)
       try:
          res = minimize(_obj_jac, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                         options={'maxiter':mi, 'ftol':1e-13, 'iprint':0})
          x=res.x
          h=project_feasible(x,n)
       except Exception:
          pass
       c=compute_c5(h)
       if c<best: best=c
       rem=...
       if rem < 30: break
    return h,best
```
Total maxiter for n=192: 630; each grad maybe 0.005 -> 3s; SLSQP internal iterations maybe multiple objective per iter? Could be 10s. Good. But if each iteration more, time may exceed. We can reduce maxits maybe. In 1300 total, 10-30s okay. For n=512: 400; okay.

But `minimize` may do many constraint evaluations; SLSQP with upper bounds and equality can be expensive. Need safe. We could use `maxiter` lower: [30,40,60,80,100] for all. But stages might not converge. Let's pick moderate:
   n<=200: [60,80,100,120,150]
   n<=300: [50,60,80,100,120]
   else: [30,40,50,60,80]
Total 186-340. Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf'); best_n=0

    # parent
    try:
       ph=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
       if ph.size>0:
          ph=project_feasible(ph, ph.size)
          best_h=ph; best_n=ph.size; best_c=compute_c5(ph)
          try:
             ec5=evaluate_erdos_solution(ph)
             if ec5 < best_c: best_c=float(ec5)
          except Exception: pass
    except Exception:
       pass

    if best_h is None:
       best_n=192
       # structured start
       start = make_sine_start(best_n, 2, 0.35, rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
       best_h=start; best_c=compute_c5(start)
       # maybe also random binary continuous? Use sine.
    
    def remaining? cannot. Use inline.
    # binary phase only if best_n even? Use parent_n from best_n if even
    binary_budget = min(0.55*float(budget_s), 750.0)
    binary_start = time.time()
    target_c = 0.38085
    # Build n_plan
    n_plan=[]
    pn=int(best_n)
    if pn%2==0 and 48<=pn<=600:
       n_plan.append((pn,0.55))
    for nn in (256,384,512):
       if not any(nn==a for a,_ in n_plan):
          if len(n_plan)<3:
             n_plan.append((nn, 0.30 if len(n_plan)==0 else 0.225))
    if not n_plan:
       n_plan=[(192,0.5),(256,0.35),(384,0.15)]
    # normalize fractions
    tot=sum(f for _,f in n_plan)
    for idx,(n,frac) in enumerate(n_plan):
       rem_bin = binary_budget - (time.time()-binary_start)
       if rem_bin < 45: break
       time_n = frac/tot * binary_budget
       if time_n > rem_bin: time_n = rem_bin
       if time_n < 20: break
       # number starts based time
       max_starts = 1 if time_n < 70 else 2 if time_n < 150 else 3
       starts = make_binary_starts(best_h, best_n, n, rng, max_starts)
       # Maybe if current best_c <= 0.379, reduce? no.
       per = time_n / len(starts)
       if per < 10:
          starts = starts[:1]; per=time_n
       for si,h0 in enumerate(starts):
          rem_total = budget_s - (time.time()-t0)
          if rem_total < 60: break
          lim = min(per - (time.time()-? per stage?), rem_total - 50)
          # use start = time.time(); but simpler lim=per
          # If other starts used time, per fixed may exceed total; use remaining for n:
          # maintain n_start? Let start_n=time at n start; time_n = frac/tot*binary_budget; but if previous over, adjust:
          # simpler: lim = min(per, max(10, binary_budget - (time.time()-binary_start)))
          lim = per
          if lim <=0: continue
          res_h,res_c = binary_sa_optimize(h0,n,lim,seed=seed+1000*si+idx)
          if res_h is not None and res_c < best_c:
              best_h=res_h.astype(float); best_c=float(res_c); best_n=n
          if best_c <= 0.3799 and len(n_plan) > idx+1:
              # break? maybe not, but binary enough; break out n_plan to continuous
              # use flag
              pass
    # continuous phase
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-t0) - 30
    if rem > 80 and best_n >= 4:
       # ensure n cont even? SLSQP can odd. But if best_n odd and binary? Use floor even maybe.
       nc = int(best_n)
       if nc %2 !=0:
          nc = max(64, nc-1)
       # Use best_h interpolated if length mismatch
       h_init = project_feasible(best_h, nc) if len(best_h)==nc else interpolate_to(best_h,len(best_h),nc)
       time1 = min(0.7*rem, 600.0)
       h1,c1 = slsqp_polish(h_init, nc, time1)
       if c1 < best_c: best_h=h1; best_c=c1; best_n=nc
       rem = budget_s - (time.time()-t0) - 25
       if rem > 60:
          # second start: sine at nc
          h2 = make_sine_start(nc, 2, 0.30, rng.rand()*2*np.pi)
          if len(best_h)==nc:
             h2 = project_feasible(0.5*h2 + 0.5*best_h, nc)
          h2,c2 = slsqp_polish(h2, nc, rem-10)
          if c2 < best_c: best_h=h2; best_c=c2; best_n=nc
    # final binary rounding and optional short SA
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-t0) - 10
    nb = int(best_n)
    if nb %2 ==0 and rem > 20:
       hb0 = round_to_binary_top(best_h, nb)
       # evaluate
       cb=compute_c5(hb0)
       if cb < best_c:
           best_c=cb; best_h=hb0.astype(float)
       # short binary SA if time
       lim = min(rem-20, 200.0)
       if lim > 20:
          hb2,cb2 = binary_sa_optimize(hb0, nb, lim, seed=seed+999)
          if cb2 < best_c:
              best_c=cb2; best_h=hb2.astype(float); best_n=nb
    # final projection/eval
    final_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c=compute_c5(final_h)
    # try evaluate if available and take min? If evaluator returns smaller, use return bound smaller? It should be exact; do.
    try:
       ec5=evaluate_erdos_solution(final_h)
       if ec5 < final_c: final_c=float(ec5)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return (final_h.astype(float), float(final_c), int(best_n))

def exact_sum_fix(h,n):
   target=0.5*n
   h=np.array(h,float)
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # choose element with room, set to target - sum_others
   remaining = target - h.sum()
   if abs(remaining)>0:
      if remaining>0: idx=np.argmax(1.0-h)
      else: idx=np.argmax(h)
      h[idx] += remaining
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
      # after clip, adjust another if needed
      remaining = target - h.sum()
      if abs(remaining)>0:
         # distribute to nearest with room
         if remaining>0:
            order=np.argsort(1.0-h)
         else:
            order=np.argsort(h)
         for i in order:
            if remaining<=0: break
            step=remaining
            if step>0:
               add=min(step,1.0-h[i])
            else:
               add=max(step,h[i])  # negative
            # Actually for step<0, add=step if h[i]+step>=0 else -h[i]
            if h[i]+add outside: add=...
            h[i]+=add
            remaining=target-h.sum()
   return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time
import math

_S_N = 0
_S_BETA = 1.0


def _eq_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * _S_N)


def _eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), dtype=float)


def _smooth_obj_jac(x, n, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - x

    c10 = np.convolve(x, b[::-1])
    c01 = np.convolve(b, x[::-1])

    P = c10[:n][::-1]
    N = c01[:n][::-1]

    if n <= 1:
        v0 = float(P[0])
        val = v0 * 2.0 / max(1, n)
        grad = (1.0 - 2.0 * x) * (2.0 / max(1, n))
        return val, grad

    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[0] = P[0]
    vals[1:n] = P[1:]
    vals[n:2 * n - 1] = N[1:]

    vmax = float(vals.max())
    s = np.clip(beta * (vals - vmax), -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(s)
    ez = float(e.sum())
    w = e / ez

    smooth = vmax + math.log(ez) / beta
    val = smooth * (2.0 / n)

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    w0 = float(w[0])
    if w0 > 1e-14:
        grad += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)

    for d in range(1, n):
        L = n - d
        wi = float(w[d])
        if wi > 1e-12:
            grad[:L] += wi * (1.0 - x[d:])
            grad[d:] += -wi * x[:L]

        wj = float(w[n + d - 1])
        if wj > 1e-12:
            grad[:L] += -wj * x[d:]
            grad[d:] += wj * (1.0 - x[:L])

    return val, grad * (2.0 / n)


def _obj_jac(x):
    return _smooth_obj_jac(x, _S_N, _S_BETA)


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    b = 1.0 - x

    c10 = np.convolve(x, b[::-1])
    c01 = np.convolve(b, x[::-1])

    m1 = float(np.max(c10[:n])) if n > 0 else 0.0
    m2 = float(np.max(c01[:n])) if n > 0 else 0.0
    return float((m1 if m1 > m2 else m2) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    y = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

    if n <= 0:
        return y

    s = float(y.sum())
    if abs(s - target) >= 1e-12:
        lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
        hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        y = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if diff > 1e-14:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
        add = min(diff, max(1.0 - y[idx], 0.0))
        y[idx] += add
        diff = target - float(y.sum())
        if diff > 1e-14:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - y)
            for i in order:
                if diff <= 1e-14:
                    break
                add = min(diff, max(1.0 - y[i], 0.0))
                y[i] += add
                diff = target - float(y.sum())
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        neg = -diff
        idx = int(np.argmax(y))
        sub = min(neg, max(y[idx], 0.0))
        y[idx] -= sub
        neg = -diff
        if neg > 1e-14:
            order = np.argsort(y)
            for i in order:
                if neg <= 1e-14:
                    break
                sub = min(neg, max(y[i], 0.0))
                y[i] -= sub
                neg = target - float(y.sum())

    return y


def interpolate_to(h, old_n, new_n):
    if old_n == new_n:
        return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if old_n <= 0 or new_n <= 0:
        return np.full(new_n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    xp = (np.arange(old_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / old_n)
    xn = (np.arange(new_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / new_n)
    return np.interp(xn, xp, np.asarray(h, dtype=float), left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))


def round_to_binary_top(h, n):
    m = n // 2
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if m <= 0:
        return out
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)

    if n == m:
        out[:] = 1
        return out

    part = np.argpartition(h, n - m)
    top = part[m:]
    out[top] = 1

    s = int(out.sum())
    if s > m:
        idx = np.where(out == 1)[0]
        order = idx[np.argsort(h[idx])]
        out[order[:s - m]] = 0
    elif s < m:
        idx = np.where(out == 0)[0]
        order = idx[np.argsort(-h[idx])]
        out[order[:m - s]] = 1

    return out


def random_binary(n, rng):
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if n > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=n // 2, replace=False)
        b[idx] = 1
    return b


def make_sine_start(n, freq, amp, phase):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    return project_feasible(y, n)


def make_binary_starts(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng, max_starts):
    starts = []
    if parent_h is not None and parent_n > 0:
        if parent_n == n:
            pc = np.asarray(parent_h, dtype=float).copy()
        else:
            pc = interpolate_to(parent_h, parent_n, n)
        starts.append(round_to_binary_top(pc, n))

    starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))

    if max_starts >= 3:
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
        ph = rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi
        starts.append(round_to_binary_top(0.5 + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 2.0 * x + ph), n))

    if max_starts >= 4:
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
        starts.append(round_to_binary_top(0.5 + 0.30 * np.cos(np.pi * x), n))

    return starts[:max_starts]


def binary_loads_int(h):
    n = len(h)
    hf = h.astype(float)
    b = 1.0 - hf
    c10 = np.convolve(hf, b[::-1])
    c01 = np.convolve(b, hf[::-1])

    q10 = np.zeros(max(n - 1, 0), dtype=np.int16)
    q01 = np.zeros(max(n - 1, 0), dtype=np.int16)

    if n > 1:
        q10[:] = np.clip(np.rint(c10[:n - 1][::-1]), 0, n).astype(np.int16)
        q01[:] = np.clip(np.rint(c01[:n - 1][::-1]), 0, n).astype(np.int16)

    return q10, q01


def apply_flip(h, p, q10, q01):
    n = len(h)
    old = int(h[p])

    if old == 0:
        L = n - p - 1
        if L > 0:
            nb = h[p + 1:]
            q10[:L] += 1 - nb
            q01[:L] -= nb
        P = p
        if P > 0:
            nb = h[:P][::-1]
            q10[:P] -= nb
            q01[:P] += 1 - nb
        h[p] = 1
    else:
        L = n - p - 1
        if L > 0:
            nb = h[p + 1:]
            q10[:L] -= 1 - nb
            q01[:L] += nb
        P = p
        if P > 0:
            nb = h[:P][::-1]
            q10[:P] += nb
            q01[:P] -= 1 - nb
        h[p] = 0


def ensure_binary_sum(h, n, rng):
    h = h.astype(np.int8).copy()
    m = n // 2
    s = int(h.sum())

    if s > m:
        idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        if idx.size:
            order = idx[rng.permutation(idx.size)[:s - m]]
            h[order] = 0
    elif s < m:
        idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            order = idx[rng.permutation(idx.size)[:m - s]]
            h[order] = 1

    return h


def build_active_positions(h, q10, q01, K):
    n = len(h)
    if K <= 0 or n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

    parts = []

    if q10.size > 0:
        top = min(8, q10.size)
        if top == q10.size:
            idx10 = np.argsort(q10)[::-1]
        else:
            idx10 = np.argpartition(q10, -top)[-top:]
        for idx in idx10:
            val = int(q10[idx])
            if val < max(1, K - 2):
                continue
            d = int(idx) + 1
            L = n - d
            cand = np.nonzero((h[:L] == 1) & (h[d:2 * d] == 0))[0]
            if cand.size:
                parts.append(cand)

    if q01.size > 0:
        top = min(8, q01.size)
        if top == q01.size:
            idx01 = np.argsort(q01)[::-1]
        else:
            idx01 = np.argpartition(q01, -top)[-top:]
        for idx in idx01:
            val = int(q01[idx])
            if val < max(1, K - 2):
                continue
            d = int(idx) + 1
            L = n - d
            cand = np.nonzero((h[:L] == 0) & (h[d:2 * d] == 1))[0]
            if cand.size:
                parts.append(cand)

    if parts:
        arr = np.concatenate(parts)
        if arr.size:
            return arr

    return np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)


def binary_sa_optimize(h0, n, time_limit, seed=1):
    if n <= 2 or int(time_limit) <= 0:
        b = ensure_binary_sum(h0, n, np.random.RandomState(seed))
        return b.astype(float), compute_c5(b.astype(float))

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed * 1000003 + n)
    h = ensure_binary_sum(h0, n, rng)
    q10, q01 = binary_loads_int(h)

    if q10.size:
        K = int(max(q10.max(), q01.max()))
    else:
        K = 0

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_K = K
    best_C = 2.0 * float(best_K) / float(n)

    end = time.time() + time_limit
    t = 2.0
    active = None
    active_timer = 0
    it = 0
    last_imp = 0

    while time.time() < end:
        it += 1

        if active is None or active_timer >= 800:
            active = build_active_positions(h, q10, q01, K)
            active_timer = 0
        else:
            active_timer += 1

        if active is not None and active.size > 0 and (it % 10 < 7):
            p = int(active[rng.randint(active.size)])
        else:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))

        bitp = int(h[p])
        q = -1
        for _ in range(30):
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if q != p and int(h[q]) != bitp:
                break

        if q < 0:
            if bitp == 0:
                opp = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
            else:
                opp = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
            if opp.size == 0:
                continue
            q = int(opp[0])
            if q == p:
                continue

        oldK = K
        apply_flip(h, p, q10, q01)
        apply_flip(h, q, q10, q01)

        m1 = int(q10.max()) if q10.size else 0
        m2 = int(q01.max()) if q01.size else 0
        newK = m1 if m1 >= m2 else m2

        accept = False
        if newK < oldK:
            accept = True
        elif newK == oldK:
            accept = rng.rand() < 0.22
        else:
            diff = newK - oldK
            if diff <= 6:
                accept = rng.rand() < math.exp(-float(diff) / t)

        if accept:
            K = newK
            if K < best_K:
                best_K = K
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_C = 2.0 * float(best_K) / float(n)
                last_imp = it
                active_timer = 800
        else:
            apply_flip(h, q, q10, q01)
            apply_flip(h, p, q10, q01)
            K = oldK

        if (it & 4095) == 0:
            q10, q01 = binary_loads_int(h)
            K = int(max(q10.max(), q01.max())) if q10.size else 0

        if it - last_imp > 2500:
            t = min(6.0, t * 1.25)
            active = None
            active_timer = 0
        else:
            t = max(0.1, t * 0.995)

    return best_h.astype(float), best_C


def slsqp_polish(h0, n, time_limit):
    global _S_N, _S_BETA

    start = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), n)
    best = compute_c5(h)

    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 6.0:
        return h, best

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _eq_con, 'jac': _eq_jac}]

    if n <= 200:
        betas = [5.0, 50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 20000.0]
        maxits = [60, 80, 100, 120, 150]
    elif n <= 300:
        betas = [5.0, 50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 20000.0]
        maxits = [50, 60, 80, 100, 120]
    else:
        betas = [5.0, 50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 20000.0]
        maxits = [30, 40, 55, 70, 90]

    for beta, mi in zip(betas, maxits):
        rem = time_limit - (time.time() - start)
        if rem < 14.0:
            break

        _S_N = int(n)
        _S_BETA = float(beta)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _obj_jac,
                h,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': int(mi), 'ftol': 1e-13, 'iprint': 0},
            )
            h = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
        except Exception:
            pass

        c = compute_c5(h)
        if c < best:
            best = c

        rem = time_limit - (time.time() - start)
        if rem < 30.0:
            break

    return h, best


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = 0
    parent_h = None
    parent_n = 0

    try:
        ph = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if ph.size > 0:
            ph = project_feasible(ph, int(ph.size))
            parent_h = ph
            parent_n = int(ph.size)
            best_h = ph.copy()
            best_n = parent_n
            best_c = compute_c5(ph)
            try:
                e = evaluate_erdos_solution(ph)
                if e is not None:
                    e = float(e)
                    if e < best_c:
                        best_c = e
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 192
        best_h = make_sine_start(best_n, 2, 0.35, rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
        parent_h = None
        parent_n = 0

    target_c = 0.38085
    binary_budget = min(0.55 * float(budget_s), 780.0)
    binary_start = time.time()

    n_plan = []
    pn = int(best_n)
    if pn % 2 == 0 and 48 <= pn <= 600:
        n_plan.append((pn, 0.55))

    for nn in (256, 384, 512):
        if not any(nn == a for a, _ in n_plan):
            if len(n_plan) < 3:
                n_plan.append((nn, 0.30 if len(n_plan) == 0 else 0.225))

    if not n_plan:
        n_plan = [(192, 0.50), (256, 0.35), (384, 0.15)]

    total_frac = sum(f for _, f in n_plan)

    for nn, frac in n_plan:
        if best_c < target_c - 1e-6:
            break

        rem_bin = binary_budget - (time.time() - binary_start)
        if rem_bin < 40.0:
            break

        time_n = (frac / total_frac) * binary_budget
        if time_n > rem_bin:
            time_n = rem_bin
        if time_n < 18.0:
            break

        max_starts = 1 if time_n < 75.0 else 2 if time_n < 150.0 else 3
        starts = make_binary_starts(best_h, best_n, nn, rng, max_starts)
        per = time_n / max(1, len(starts))
        if per < 12.0:
            starts = starts[:1]
            per = time_n

        for si, h0 in enumerate(starts):
            if best_c < target_c - 1e-6:
                break
            rem_total = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - t0)
            if rem_total < 60.0:
                break

            lim = min(per, rem_total - 50.0)
            if lim <= 8.0:
                break

            hs, cs = binary_sa_optimize(h0, nn, lim, seed=seed + 1000 * si + nn)
            if hs is not None and cs < best_c:
                best_h = hs.astype(float).copy()
                best_n = nn
                best_c = float(cs)

    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - t0) - 35.0
    if rem > 80.0 and best_n >= 4:
        nc = int(best_n)
        if nc % 2 != 0:
            nc = max(64, nc - 1)

        h_init = best_h.copy() if len(best_h) == nc else interpolate_to(best_h, len(best_h), nc)
        h_init = project_feasible(h_init, nc)

        time1 = min(0.70 * rem, 650.0)
        h1, c1 = slsqp_polish(h_init, nc, time1)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h = h1.copy()
            best_n = nc
            best_c = float(c1)

        rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - t0) - 25.0
        if rem > 70.0:
            h2 = make_sine_start(nc, 2, 0.30, rng.rand() * 2.0 * np.pi)
            if len(best_h) == nc:
                h2 = project_feasible(0.55 * h2 + 0.45 * best_h, nc)
            h2, c2 = slsqp_polish(h2, nc, rem - 12.0)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2.copy()
                best_n = nc
                best_c = float(c2)

    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - t0) - 12.0
    nb = int(best_n)
    if nb % 2 == 0 and rem > 25.0:
        hb0 = round_to_binary_top(best_h, nb)
        cb0 = compute_c5(hb0.astype(float))
        if cb0 < best_c:
            best_h = hb0.astype(float).copy()
            best_c = float(cb0)
            best_n = nb

        lim = min(rem - 20.0, 260.0)
        if lim > 25.0:
            hb, cb = binary_sa_optimize(hb0, nb, lim, seed=seed + 777777)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb.astype(float).copy()
                best_n = n
                best_c = float(cb)

    final_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(final_h)

    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(final_h)
        if ec is not None:
            ec = float(ec)
            if ec < final_c:
                final_c = ec
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (final_h.astype(float), float(final_c), int(best_n))
```
